# T3 Code support matrix: t3code v0.1.2 against Mend's gateway

This matrix grades every part of T3 Code that a t3code client can reach through Mend's
`apps/t3-gateway`: each RPC method, each orchestration command, each config, capability and auth
field, each projection field and turn-item kind, and each control in the web client. For every row
that does not work as t3code expects, it says whether the gateway can close the gap alone or Mend
needs a core change, and how large that change is. It is the work list for the roadmap item "T3 Code
gateway: 100% of T3 Code". `docs/adr/0012-t3code-gateway.md` describes the gateway, and the docs
page `apps/docs/src/content/docs/integrations/t3code.md` tells a person what works today.

## Summary

The #1491 box check on preview 128 observed default-branch text/search before a session, absolute
worktree paths and their refusals, all 20,030 paging entries, and automatic icons. Draft image and
HTML previews failed because `assets.createUrl` omitted `draft-workspace-file`. The correction
resolves its visible `cwd` to the default branch or Local worktree and retains per-load access
checks. Gateway coverage opens an image and HTML with relative CSS before any worktree exists; the
corrected draft previews await the independent box recheck.
[Box evidence](https://github.com/sealant-sh/mend/pull/1491#issuecomment-6714118838).

Regraded on 2026-11-05 against two pinned sources:

- Mend `origin/main` at
  [f743fe34548912e9d4d4027271a991d91d6f823f](https://github.com/sealant-sh/mend/tree/f743fe34548912e9d4d4027271a991d91d6f823f).
  Every row that was PARTIAL or MISSING was checked against this source, including rows whose grade
  stayed the same. Their Gateway cells carry current evidence. Previously supported rows retain
  their historical citations unless the regrade updates them. The counts below come from the
  detailed rows, rather than the previous summary.
- t3code `v0.1.2`
  ([044f07257c9684ae4543bd1e1ebf9c394e9e3830](https://github.com/pingdotgg/t3code/tree/044f07257c9684ae4543bd1e1ebf9c394e9e3830)),
  the gateway's checked-in contracts pin. The previous pin, `v0.1.1-preview.20261017.3254`
  (b2bab46ec), marks what is `NEW v0.1.2` or `CHANGED v0.1.2`. The older markers `NEW v0.1.1` and
  `CHANGED v0.1.1` compare v0.1.1 with the pin before it, `v0.0.46-nightly.20261010.2922`.

The v0.1.2 pin is merged in mend#1132. Native steering is merged in mend#1198, #1342 and #1354. Raw
worktree reads are merged in #1139 and #1153, scratch and new-by-name projects in #1214, and
worktree and project removal in #1227 and #1253. Agent pages and inline proof captures are merged in
#1170 and #1334. The launch and streaming fixes from the 2026-11-02 timing pass are merged in #1373,
#1374 and #1376 through #1381. A remaining row is graded for the behavior still missing, not for a
route or feature that these changes already shipped.

The existing gaps were source-audited. The four stop-status rows were then regraded against
mend#1425's protocol fix at
[1c3f756d4e](https://github.com/sealant-sh/mend/commit/1c3f756d4ee8a85985eb253acecf49ffb7bfe247) and
gateway reconciliation at
[f4e75c48d2](https://github.com/sealant-sh/mend/commit/f4e75c48d21d15e58297d2f7da476e54881a0595),
based on main `adb1be105a`. Both Stop generation and session Stop were verified with the real v0.1.2
client against an isolated local API and gateway with fake logins. Session Stop closes real and
still-starting provisional runs, stays idle after reload, and allows an ordinary follow-up to
relaunch and answer without reload (SLNT-527). Those rows are IMPLEMENTED with this fix.

SLNT-534 adds same-provider model and effort snapshots for new turns after that audit. Its evidence
is the adapter, host and gateway tests named below. The independent real-client check remains a
merge requirement; these changes do not regrade the separate stop rows.

SLNT-538 implements native Plan/default interaction, durable proposed plans and Refine/Implement
flows for Codex and Claude. Evidence is the domain, protocol, host and gateway tests; the parent
owns the independent v0.1.2 client check. Save-to-file and todo-list plans remain Part2. The
implementation adds no migration and does not include the independent startup-stop PR #1437.

The first box correction for SLNT-538 permits Claude's own native plan-file Write/Edit during an
admitted Plan turn under ask and bypass. The protocol adapter limits the exception to a native
classifier-approvable alias check; the executor verifies its managed directory, regular file and
exact path from the conversation's native plan attachment. It restores this scope from recorded turn
selections on reattachment. Unknown metadata and other safety objections still ask, as do Supervised
project writes. Local real-CLI 2.1.318 probes use a fake Anthropic endpoint; protocol, host and
filesystem tests cover permissions and replay. Parent verification with the pinned client and fake
logins observed Plan, Refine and Implement under both Claude permission stances. Plan-file
Write/Edit opened no approval; Supervised Default still opened a project Write approval. The
executor resolves the workspace's physical directory before locating Claude's native transcript,
covering Mend's linked worktree mount. Captured-store LIVE remains BLOCKED (VPN). A separate local
Core replay-boundary mismatch blocked the API-restart follow-up check and is tracked as SLNT-542 in
`PLATFORM-FEEDBACK.md`. Independent box round 2 remains required. These results do not change the
grades below.

SLNT-541 implements fork only, including the merged Plan turn selections and native events.
`SessionEngine.forkSession`, PostgreSQL migration 0194, `native-history.ts` and the gateway's `fork`
command retain an exact checkpoint/native cutoff and one durable child receipt. Fork creation
launches no provider. Its first message uses the ordinary child bootstrap and the asker's own login,
binds the new native identity, then dispatches. Local protocol, lifecycle, store and gateway
command/projection tests cover this path in parts; fake-only Codex 0.162.1 and Claude 2.1.318 probes
observed exact native cutoff, no creation prompt, unchanged source history and independent
parent/child resume. The real pinned v0.1.2 client against an isolated local API and gateway with
fake logins exposed Fork after the first response for both providers. Codex's child answered with
the inherited native context. Claude restored an older selected checkpoint without later source
input, answered after source deletion, then resumed after Stop and reload. PostgreSQL repository and
notification tests passed. Captured-store LIVE remains BLOCKED (VPN); local client proof used the
colocated store and held Core next.786 with SDK next.792. The independent box check remains
required. Claude 2.1.318 Plan files, inline todos, session task files and persisted text outputs are
retained at the selected boundary. Native subagents, shared task lists and unverified auxiliary
layouts refuse. Inherited display retains message text and provenance without child runs or costs;
rich inherited tool items and the source-side `fork` item remain missing. The existing Plan
limitation is unchanged. Merge-back is described below.

Checkpoint rollback now prepares an independent native replacement for Codex and Claude. It saves
recovery before changing managed files or conversation history, fences the worktree and publishes a
new history revision without rewriting original execution evidence. Actual PostgreSQL, real Git,
public SDK fakes, native adapter tests and the pinned reducer/Undo waiter cover the implementation.
The shared path checks the actual workspace login holder, including joined sessions; another
person's shared-home provider operation refuses before admission. Captured LIVE remains BLOCKED
(VPN). The local pinned-client cases and remaining independent box gate are recorded below.

Decision A uses ordinary native fork bootstrap in the verified shared holder's existing `/root`
login home, with immutable selected seed staging and a distinct native candidate identity.
Per-person replacements retain isolated revision homes. Durable bindings identify the placement used
by resume, continuation and later rollback. Local PostgreSQL and SDK-boundary fixtures cover both
providers, colocated shared and captured shared/person layouts, exact holder/launch checks before
effects, unchanged source files, subsequent sends and second rollback. Recovery across new service
instances preserves both binding locations. Native recovery scopes Claude auxiliary files to the
selected identity and Codex ancestors to verified byte/ordinal prefixes. Other sessions in the
shared home are excluded. Missing or modified retained history refuses.

Parent verification used actual Codex 0.162.1 and Claude 2.1.318 with loopback fake providers
through the pinned v0.1.2 client at `044f07257c`, against local Mend and Core next.794 / SDK
next.792 in colocated mode. Both providers retained the selected input, withdrew later input,
answered once after reload, Stop and API restart, and completed a second rollback with a new native
identity. Claude covered file restoration and preserving a person's local edit. Codex covered source
transcript deletion and a paginated native ancestor. Evidence is retained at
`/tmp/mend-rollback-delivery.YWAf4J/`. These cases do not establish live-provider, captured-store,
fresh-workspace capture restore or box passes. Captured LIVE remains `BLOCKED(VPN)`.

A later Codex Undo to origin hit a filed Core gap: recorded exec stdout redacted a token-shaped
match inside the saved Git pack's base64. Mend refused before changing files or history and kept the
original recovery. The regression checks its plain refusal and retry of that same saved Undo. Exact
workspace-file bytes outside stdout are requested in `PLATFORM-FEEDBACK.md`; no encoding or transfer
workaround was added. Independent box verification remains required. SLNT-543 stays parked.

Merge-back follows the confirmed native v0.1.2 behavior. PostgreSQL 0202 retains the selected
child's own context, its direct parent and a keyed receipt. A newer pending transfer from that child
replaces the earlier one. The ordinary parent dispatch assigns context once to an authored turn and
leaves its visible message, selection and payer unchanged. Running parents continue; queued
cancellation before dispatch and Stop/Resume retain pending context. Gateway RPC and actual
PostgreSQL/provider-wire tests cover replacement, access revocation, source deletion, dispatch
retry, waiting selections and handoff projections after reload. Box verification remains required.

Each row has one grade. IMPLEMENTED works as t3code expects. PARTIAL works with a gap a person can
notice, and the row names the gap. MISSING is absent, refused or silent. OUT-OF-MEND is something
only T3's own cloud provides. Every PARTIAL or MISSING row has a scope: `client limitation` when the
pinned client must change, `gateway-only` (buildable in `apps/t3-gateway` over Mend's existing HTTP
API, with the routes named), `core` (a Mend engine, API or store change, sized S for a route over
existing engine state, M for new engine behaviour in one package, L for a cross-package change), or
`rule` (Mend refuses it by ADR 0007). A row a decision of 2026-10-24 settles says "Decided
2026-10-24 (decision N)" and keeps the grade its source earns. A decision never makes a row
IMPLEMENTED.

| Section                                  | IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded | gateway-only | core | rule | client limitation | per-command |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ------ | ------------ | ---- | ---- | ----------------- | ----------- |
| §A RPC methods                           | 53          | 18      | 125     | 2           | 198    | 26           | 115  | 1    | 0                 | 1           |
| §B Orchestration commands                | 35          | 7       | 7       | 0           | 49     | 3            | 11   | 0    | 0                 | 0           |
| §C Config, capabilities, auth            | 79          | 10      | 33      | 3           | 125    | 16           | 26   | 0    | 1                 | 0           |
| §D Projection                            | 88          | 18      | 19      | 0           | 125    | 21           | 16   | 0    | 0                 | 0           |
| §E1 Sidebar, menus, palette, keybindings | 52          | 6       | 10      | 0           | 68     | 8            | 8    | 0    | 0                 | 0           |
| §E2 Composer, thread view, launch        | 60          | 15      | 14      | 0           | 89     | 6            | 23   | 0    | 0                 | 0           |
| §E3 Right panel                          | 28          | 18      | 40      | 1           | 87     | 27           | 29   | 2    | 0                 | 0           |
| §E4 Settings, onboarding, pairing        | 25          | 2       | 33      | 1           | 61     | 11           | 24   | 0    | 0                 | 0           |
| **Total**                                | 420         | 94      | 281     | 7           | 802    | 118          | 252  | 3    | 1                 | 1           |

The sections overlap on purpose: a UI row in §E and the RPC or field behind it in §A to §D are both
counted, so the total counts rows, not distinct features. Inside a section, each feature has one
row. The 75 rows that graded a feature a second time are listed under their section as
cross-references and are not counted. The 14 rows for what v0.1.2 removed are listed under their
section as removed and are not counted either.

### Project operations update, 2026-11-07

URL adoption, GitHub repository lookup on the caller's own login, source-control discovery and
project rename are implemented in `project-operations.ts` in the gateway and API. HTTP, Postgres and
paired WebSocket tests cover these paths; a pinned-client box check is still pending. Clone
tracking, cancellation and retry remain deferred. T3 uses its blocking clone flow and spinner. The
GitHub picker mirrors Mend's own picker: public repositories use HTTPS and private repositories use
SSH with the person's configured Mend key or bridge. Free-form display titles, T3's GitHub account
editor, folder import, publish without origin and cross-thread search remain open.

### Gaps the gateway can close alone

These 27 features need no Mend core change. Each is sized for one pull request against
`apps/t3-gateway`, and each names the Mend routes it reads or calls.

1. **Settlement on pull requests, and settled cleanup.** Pins, thread order, the opt-out and the
   three-day sweep are served since mend#1068. What remains: settle a thread whose change's pull
   request merged or closed, as t3code's default `sidebarAutoSettleOnMerge` does, from
   `GET /api/sessions/:id/landings`, `GET /api/changes/:id/landings` and
   `POST /api/landings/:id/refresh`, and stop a settled thread's session on a timer
   (`POST /api/sessions/:id/stop`). It never deletes a worktree. Rows: B.26, C.E26, E1a.18 (3).
2. **A per-person settings home.** Model preferences (mend#1055) and project actions (mend#1064) are
   the only settings patches the gateway takes today. What remains: the environment icon,
   per-project providers, the auto-settle settings, and the server rows Mend can carry
   (`GET /api/settings`, `PUT /api/settings`, `/api/me/notifications`, `/api/me/git-author`).
   t3code's `settings:write` is all or nothing on the client, so once the gateway grants it, it must
   serve or refuse every patch key by name. Rows: A.29, C.E28, C.S15, E4.2, E4.3, E4.20, E4.N8 (7).
3. **Keybinding edits.** Per-person rules in gateway state, served as `keybindings` with
   `keybindingsUpdated`. "Open keybindings.json" and "Open logs folder" get a refusal that says why.
   No Mend route. Rows: A.23, A.24, C.C4, C.C5, C.C11, E4.7, E4.8, E4.12 (8).
4. **Pull request list and inbox.** `GET /api/projects/:id/pull-requests` and `GET /api/projects`
   behind `pullRequests.list`, `listStats`, `summary`, `routing`, `git.resolvePullRequest` and the
   composer `#` picker, with the `pullRequests` capability. Rows: A.71, A.72, A.73, A.74, A.75,
   A.138, E1b.r62, E3.41, E3.48, E3.49, E3.50, E3.54 (12).
5. **Pull requests on threads.** A session's landings (`GET /api/sessions/:id/landings`,
   `GET /api/changes/:id/landings`, `POST /api/landings/:id/refresh`) as the thread's pull request:
   the sidebar badge, the Linked tab, PR search terms and `thread.pull-request-link-updated`. The
   git panel already reads the landing's pull request since mend#1065. The thread shell does not.
   Links a person adds are kept in gateway state. Rows: A.77, B.32, C.E16, D.20, D.E3, E1a.29, E3.8,
   E3.52, E3.56 (9).
6. **Check out a same-repository pull request.** `headRefName` from
   `GET /api/projects/:id/pull-requests`, then `POST /api/projects/:id/refresh`,
   `POST /api/projects/:id/worktrees` with that base, and `POST /api/worktrees/:id/sessions`. A
   fork's head needs a core change (A.139). Row: E3.47.
7. **Review guide from Mend's tour.** `GET /api/changes/:id/tour` and `POST /api/changes/:id/tour`
   for a pull request Mend landed. It is reachable only once the pull request panel is served. Rows:
   A.82, A.83, E3.NG1 (3).
8. **Clone tracking and GitHub account editing.** Blocking URL/GitHub adoption and discovery are
   implemented on 2026-11-07. Tracking/progress/retry remain open; cancellation needs a Mend API.
   Account editing can use the person's connected-account APIs. Adding a project from a folder needs
   a transport design (decision 9). Rows: A.106, A.108, C.E21, E1a.36, E4.27 (5).
9. **Create a worktree without launching a thread.** `POST /api/projects/:id/worktrees` behind
   `vcs.createWorktree`. Worktree and project removal are supported, including Mend's checks and the
   restorable project removal in mend#1253. The remaining part of `projects.mutate` is folder-based
   creation and project settings beyond rename. Row: A.141.
10. **Provider subagents and Claude workflows.** Claude `Agent`/`Task` calls and `task` items and
    Codex `collabAgentToolCall` items from `GET /api/sessions/:id/items` as `subagents[]` with
    `workflow`, and a stop through `POST /api/sessions/:id/waiting/end`. Mend now retains Claude
    child-item parents and delegation results (mend#1423). Opening a separate child conversation
    still needs core work. Rows: A.186, B.40, D.60, D.N4, D.99, D.101, D.E6, E2b.N3, E2b.N4, E2b.47
    (10).
11. **Todo checklists.** Claude `TodoWrite` and Codex `plan` and `todoList` items from
    `GET /api/sessions/:id/items` as `plans[]`, `todo_list` items and `plan.updated`. Rows: D.70,
    D.83, D.E9, E2b.48 (4).
12. **Background work.** `GET /api/sessions/:id/waiting` and running `task` items as
    `pendingBackgroundTasks`, and the banner's Stop through `POST /api/sessions/:id/waiting/end`.
    Rows: D.36, D.63, E2b.13 (3).
13. **Projection fidelity.** From `GET /api/sessions/:id/turns` and `GET /api/sessions/:id/items`:
    the context meter at each turn's end, a harness-opened turn as a `notification`, interrupt
    markers, checkpoint items, file-change counts, file-search items, the latest run's error, work
    start times and bounded snapshots. Rows: D.27, D.34, D.57a, D.64, D.69, D.77, D.81, D.85, D.87,
    D.90, D.91, C.P5 (12). Completed-turn usage already reaches the API; the gateway's turn decoder
    drops it. Live usage remains a core gap.
14. **Command output streaming.** `orchestration.subscribeCommandOutput` from the item text the hub
    republishes (`GET /api/sessions/:id/items`), and the `commandOutputStreaming` capability. Rows:
    A.199, C.E29, D.E16, E2b.N5 (4).
15. **Inline message context, implemented.** Structured records and authorized thread transcript
    snapshots reach normal sends, queued edits, steers, launches and pending answers. Raw composer
    readback survives reopen. Gateway/WebSocket coverage is in `message-context.test.ts`; box
    verification remains required. Rows: C.E8, E2a.17, E2a.22, E2a.23 (4).
16. **Open in an editor over Remote-SSH.** `remoteOpenTargets` from `GET /api/workspace-ssh`. Rows:
    A.118, C.C9, E1b.r50, E3.21 (4).
17. **Repository identity and prompt links.** `originUrl` from `GET /api/projects/:id` as
    `repositoryIdentity`, which also groups one repository across environments and shows v0.1.2's
    "Add to another machine". Ignored-file copying is deferred: captured reads omit ignored files
    and copying needs a live workspace listing and transfer path. Rows: A.V2, D.3, E1b.N3, E3.V2,
    E4.N9 (5).
18. **Favicons and absolute paths.** `filesystem.getMetadata` and fresh listings are served since
    mend#1063 and mend#1070. What remains: favicon discovery in the project's files, and a file read
    at an absolute path inside one of the person's worktrees, mapped as `getMetadata` maps it. Over
    `GET /api/projects/:id/files` and `GET /api/worktrees/:id/contents`. Raw worktree reads, agent
    pages and proof media are supported; other image resource kinds still need adapters. Rows: D.7,
    E3.34, E3.38 (3). Project default-branch bytes remain core work under A.123 and E3.NP4.
19. **Several models at once.** `requiredWorktreeBootstrap`, and one session per model through
    `POST /api/projects/:id/sessions`. Rows: C.E9, E2b.29.
20. **Launch fidelity.** Start from origin, a retry of a failed preparation and 100 images per
    message are served since mend#1064 and mend#1070. Signed image uploads are served by mend#1474.
    What remains: `thread.create` mapped to a launch (`POST /api/projects/:id/sessions`) and the
    resume turn without synthetic text. Rows: B.37, E2a.6 (2).
21. **"Local" as a shared worktree.** One worktree per project on the default branch, made once with
    `POST /api/projects/:id/local-worktree` and kept by `ProjectLocalWorktrees`, then
    `POST /api/worktrees/:id/sessions` for each "Local" thread, as decision 12 reads "Local". It
    also makes the setup card's "Work locally" work. Rows: B.47, D.2, E2b.3, E2b.70 (4).
22. **Project scripts.** Actions are kept per person and project and served as the shell's `scripts`
    since mend#1064. What remains: the Project settings page through `projectSettingsOverrides`, the
    environment-wide default actions, and t3code's environment variables in the shell over
    `/api/tty`. Rows: C.S8, E1a.41, E2b.61 (3).
23. **Diff and git menu fidelity.** Ignore-whitespace on "Changes" through
    `GET /api/worktrees/:id/diff`, the behind count from `GET /api/changes/:id/landings?probe=true`,
    and a no-op pick of the thread's own branch. Rows: E2b.57, E3.17, E3.NG2 (3).
24. **Desktop preview bookkeeping and discovered servers.** Preview sessions kept per thread for the
    Electron app, URLs and local servers from `GET /api/services` and
    `GET /api/sessions/:id/processes`. Rows: A.169, E3.6, E3.57, E3.58, E3.59, E3.60, E3.61 (7).
25. **Authorized clients.** `GET /api/me/devices`, `DELETE /api/me/devices/:id` and
    `POST /api/me/devices/pairings` behind `subscribeAuthAccess`. Rows: A.181, E4.18.
26. **Provider account details and sign-out.** `GET /api/me/sealant` already returns the person's
    account names, credential kinds and available identity metadata. Project those details and map
    sign-out to `DELETE /api/me/sealant/accounts/:id`. Rows: A.14, C.P16, C.P17, E2a.45 (4).
27. **Host stall notice.** The gateway measures its own event-loop stalls and sends the latest as
    v0.1.2's `lastEventLoopStall`. No Mend route. Rows: C.V1, E4.V6.

Environment themes are a client limitation. Appearance reads themes only from T3's primary
environment in pinned v0.1.2 and nightly `39c5e43db307`. A paired Mend environment cannot add its
palettes to the menu. #1506 is closed; a T3 client change is required. Row: C.C19.

### Gaps that need a Mend core change

These 60 changes are grouped by the change, not by the row. The size is the change's. A row in the
group may size its own slice smaller.

**Turns and the agent**

- **Remaining live options (M).** Service tier at launch and per turn, an in-session permission
  change, `auto-accept-edits` and `auto` modes, and persistent "always allow" remain. Same-provider
  model and effort apply on the next new native turn in SLNT-534. Rows: A.195, B.5, B.13, B.22,
  E2a.42, E2a.43 (6).
- **Provider switch in one thread (L).** A cross-harness resume in protocol mode, with the distilled
  conversation as the opening turn, after live model and effort (decision 2). Rows: B.36, C.P3,
  D.74, D.95, D.E11, E2a.39 (6).
- **Plan mode.** SLNT-538 implements launch and per-turn interaction snapshots, Claude acknowledged
  controls and denied ExitPlanMode capture, Codex collaboration modes and plan deltas, typed durable
  proposals, and Refine/Implement here/new-thread paths (decision 3). Rows B.34, B.37, C.P4, D.17,
  D.35, D.57c, D.71, D.82, E2a.27 and E2a.28 are implemented. E2b.39 remains partial: file saving
  belongs to Part2. D.E9 emits proposed-plan updates; its todo-list branch also remains Part2.
- **Fork, rollback and merge back (L).** SLNT-541 adds exact-boundary fork with retained native
  seeds and child provenance. Claude 2.1.318 Plan, inline todo, session task-file and persisted text
  output closure is covered by native fake-provider probes and captured/colocated delivery tests.
  B.42 and E2b.40 remain partial for native subagents and gateway follow-ups; D.21 is implemented
  and D.96's source fork item is still missing. Rollback saves recovery before replacing native
  history and optionally restoring files (decision 4). Merge-back saves selected child context for
  the direct parent's next message. Open rows: B.42, D.96, E2b.40 (3).
- **Delegated tasks and the orchestrator MCP (L).** An orchestrator MCP for Mend's agents first,
  then a session that starts, waits on and supervises child sessions, with a completion mailbox, and
  T3 Code's part (decision 5). Peer links are gone at v0.1.2. Rows: B.44, D.57b, D.97, E1.V2, E4.50
  (5).
- **Usage-limit stops (M).** Adapters classify a failure and `AgentTurn` records the class and the
  reset time, so limit recovery can resume at reset, and Codex's error code passed on. Rows: B.18,
  D.28, D.29, D.30, D.93, D.V3, E2b.72 (7).
- **Live token usage (M).** Publish in-flight Codex `thread/tokenUsage/updated` and Claude usage on
  the run. Completed-turn usage is already on the turns route and `provider-turn.updated` is
  emitted; the end-of-turn meter needs a gateway adapter. Rows: D.67, E2a.44 (2).
- **Approval details (M).** Preserve the provider's approval reason and ask-rule override metadata
  across the adapter boundary, then project them into T3 Code's approval panel. Row: D.89.
- **Idle compaction (M).** Native manual and pre-turn compaction are implemented; automatic idle
  scheduling remains deferred. Rows: C.V8, E4.V1 (2).
- **Provider slash commands (M).** The harness's command list per workspace and a way to run one.
  Rows: C.P22, E2a.29.
- **Codex goal (M).** The thread goal recorded on the session. Rows: D.38, D.66.
- **Secret requests (M).** An agent-facing secret prompt recorded as a request kind, answered
  without the record keeping the secret. Rows: A.63, D.98, E2b.73 (3).
- **MCP apps (L).** MCP tool calls with UI resources on Mend's items, and a proxy to the app's
  server. Rows: A.127, A.128, A.129, A.130, E2b.46 (5).
- **Workflow scripts (L).** Claude workflow files kept where `getWorkflowScript` can read them.
  Rows: A.185, E2b.49.
- **Restart continuation (M).** A turn cut by a Mend restart runs again after it. Whether the engine
  already does this was not checked. Row: E4.6.
- **Skills checked into the repository (S).** Skills the harness finds in the worktree itself,
  listed per session beside the skills Mend delivers (mend#1070). Row: E2a.30.
- **Thread titles on demand (S).** `POST /api/sessions/:id/label/generate` over the existing namer.
  Rows: B.16, C.E14, D.13, E1a.19 (4).
- **Text generation (M).** A route that answers t3code's writing asks with Mend's inference on the
  asker's own login. Rows: C.P9, E4.4.

**Files, worktrees and git**

- **Project default-branch assets (S).** Raw bytes for project favicons and draft files still need a
  read against the project's default branch. Raw worktree and checkpoint reads, pages and proof
  media are supported. Other T3 resource kinds need gateway adapters. Rows: A.123, E3.NP4 (2).
- **Reads at the project's default branch (S).** A file read and a content search over the bare
  store, for draft threads and the project root. Rows: A.111, A.112, E3.27 (3).
- **Listing pages (S).** Paging and directory-scoped listing in `GET /api/projects/:id/files`. Rows:
  A.110, A.113.
- **File writes (L).** `PUT /api/worktrees/:id/contents` for the change's owner or under shared
  control, captured workspaces included. Rows: A.116, E3.28, E3.35 (3).
- **Non-image attachments (M).** Any file placed in the workspace beside images, on messages and on
  question answers. Rows: A.125, B.6, C.E7, E2a.14, E2a.15, E2a.16, E2a.19 (7).
- **Working-tree status (M).** HEAD, index, uncommitted files and upstream ahead and behind over the
  API, and the uncommitted diff. Since mend#1063 a worktree with change files is never called clean.
  Capture-backed sessions make it L. Rows: A.132, A.136, A.146, E3.13, E3.NG3 (5).
- **Diff against a project ref (S).** A project ref as `from` on `GET /api/worktrees/:id/diff`. Row:
  E3.16.
- **Commit.** Standalone commit, selected files and custom/generated messages now use
  `POST /api/worktrees/:id/commit` as the owner. Writer effort/Fast options remain partial because
  the SDK accepts only a model. Custom selections/messages on stacked push/PR actions remain
  refused. Rows: A.137, E3.43a.
- **Pull.** `POST /api/worktrees/:id/pull` runs `git pull --ff-only` as the owner, matching pinned
  T3. Ahead/behind status still needs the comparison-fidelity slice for the automatic menu choice
  and divergence hint. Rows: A.135, E3.43d, E3.43e (3).
- **Branch switch and create in a session's worktree (L).** A store `checkoutBranch` and
  `POST /api/sessions/:id/branch` for the owner of a worktree with one session and no landing in
  flight, after ADR 0007 is amended (decision 10). Rows: A.143, A.144, B.17, E3.43g, E3.NG4 (5).
- **A pull request's head from a fork (M).** Fetch `refs/pull/N/head` into the store and base a
  worktree on it. Row: A.139.
- **Publish a repository (M).** `gh repo create` and a push of the default branch as the person.
  Rows: A.105, E3.45.
- **Storage cleanup and worktree retention (M).** A report and a run in `packages/store`, with the
  keep-when guards from the working-tree status. Rows: A.25, C.E18, C.S11, E4.9, E4.N2 (5).
- **Editor launch on Mend's machine (S).** Real `availableEditors`, useful only when that machine
  has a display. Row: E3.37.

**Projects**

- **Add a project from the person's machine (L).** T3 Code sends only a path and the gateway runs on
  Mend's server, so the repository comes from the person's machine, as `mend handoff` carries a
  checkout. Never from a path on Mend's server (decision 9). Rows: A.119, E1a.31, E1a.32 (3).
- **Project rename and icon (S).** `PUT /api/projects/:id/name` and icon fields with a route. Rows:
  A.117, E1a.37, E1a.38 (3).
- **Project defaults (M).** A per-project default model and runtime mode. Row: E1a.43.
- **Other forges (M).** GitLab, Forgejo, Bitbucket and Azure providers beside `gh`. Row: E1a.34b.
- **Cancel an adoption (S).** Stop a clone in flight. Row: A.107.
- **Import agent sessions (L).** Scan the person's Codex and Claude history and import it as
  projects and sessions. Rows: A.122, E4.45.

**Pull requests**

- **Pull request read model (L).** Detail, checks, timeline, review threads, files, candidates,
  viewed state, v0.1.2's change breakdown and a refresh feed, read as the person over GitHub. Rows:
  A.76, A.78, A.79, A.80, A.81, A.84, A.85, A.86, A.96, A.97, A.98, A.99, A.101, A.V1, E2a.18, E3.7,
  E3.9, E3.51, E3.55, E3.V1 (20).
- **Pull request writes (L).** Comment, review, reply, resolve, react, reviewers, labels, viewed
  files and the pull request actions through the person's `gh`. Rows: A.87, A.88, A.90, A.91, A.92,
  A.93, A.94, A.95, A.100, A.102 (10).
- **Merge a person presses (M).** A merge of the change's pull request when a person presses Merge
  in T3 Code, with the merge method and the agent-credit setting. Mend never merges on its own,
  automatic landing included, and never force-pushes. ADR 0007's "Mend never merges" is amended for
  this (decision 13). Rows: C.E17, C.S16, E2b.N10, E4.26 (4).
- **Pull request watch (M).** A worker that refreshes a landing's pull request and wakes the agent
  on a failed check, a review or a comment. Rows: B.33, E3.53.

**Search, usage and limits**

- **Message search (M).** `GET /api/sessions/search` over the person's session items. Rows: A.190,
  E1a.30.
- **Usage summary (M).** Tokens and cost per harness and model from `agent_turns.usage`, with a
  price table, on the asker's own login (decision 7). Rows: A.53, A.54, C.E19, C.S10, E3.67, E3.68,
  E3.71 (7).
- **Limits (M).** Rate-limit windows read through each person's harness login (Codex
  `account/rateLimits`, Claude limit events), after the usage summary. Spending a reset credit makes
  it L and comes last (decision 7). Rows: A.5, C.C20, C.P25, E2a.35, E3.69, E3.70, E4.40 (7).

**Harnesses and logins**

- **Provider sign-in (L).** A login flow Mend runs as the person (start, complete, respond, cancel,
  progress), stored where `mend connect` stores it. Rows: A.6, A.7, A.8, A.9, A.10, A.11, A.12,
  A.13, A.15, C.P10, E4.36, E4.38, E4.47, E4.59 (14).
- **Harness configuration (M).** Per-person enable, display name, environment and custom models.
  Rows: A.4, E4.35, E4.48 (3).
- **Harness install and update (L).** The planned auto-updating harness feature, with versions and
  advisories. Rows: A.16, A.17, A.18, A.19, C.P13, C.P26, E4.39 (7).
- **More harnesses (L).** pi and opencode as protocol sessions, and an ACP registry. Rows: A.35,
  A.36, A.37, A.38, A.39, A.40, A.41, A.42, A.43, A.44, A.45, C.C7, E4.37 (13).
- **Feedback upload (M).** `/feedback` run in the session's workspace on the person's login. Rows:
  A.131, E2a.34.

**Instance and operations**

- **Preview browser (L).** A server-side browser per session streamed to the client, and agent
  browser tools. Rows: A.158, A.159, A.160, A.161, A.162, A.163, A.164, A.165, A.166, A.167, C.E22,
  E2b.63, E3.5, E3.NP6, E4.23 (15).
- **Device hub (L).** iOS simulators and Android emulators per host, and v0.1.2's EAS cloud hosts
  with their Expo account. Rows: A.170, A.171, A.172, A.173, A.174, A.175, A.176, A.177, A.V3, C.V9,
  E3.10, E3.62, E3.63, E3.64, E3.V3, E4.24, E4.V4 (17).
- **Scheduled sessions (L).** Cron, interval and webhook triggers on pg-boss, a webhook ingress with
  v0.1.2's request filters and dedupe, and delivery logs. Rows: A.58, A.59, A.60, A.61, A.62, A.64,
  A.65, E4.32 (8).
- **Server diagnostics (M).** Traces, process tree and resource telemetry over the API, and a signal
  for a listed process. Rows: A.46, A.47, A.49, A.50, A.51, A.55, A.183, E3.75, E4.10, E4.11 (10).
- **Host resources (S).** CPU and memory on the API. Row: A.48.
- **Telemetry export (M).** OTLP endpoints for Mend's server, operator only. Rows: A.52, C.E25,
  E4.N3 (3).
- **Server self-update (L).** A route that runs `mend server upgrade` with progress. Rows: A.20,
  A.21, C.E20 (3).
- **Landing writing policy (M).** Branch naming and commit and pull request text per person. Row:
  C.S13.
- **Review guide settings (M).** t3code's guide settings served from Mend's machine review pass.
  Row: C.S14.
- **A per-person settings model (M).** For the remaining server settings Mend adopts, one per
  behaviour. Row: C.S17.

### Out of Mend

These 7 rows need T3's own cloud. Mend cannot provide them, however large the work.

- A.69 `cloud.getRelayClientStatus` and A.70 `cloud.installRelayClient`: the T3 Connect relay is
  T3's cloud service.
- C.E23 `agentActivityPublishing`: it publishes agent activity to T3 Connect.
- C.E31 `linkRevoked`: it is a T3 Connect account link.
- C.V6 the T3 Connect reachability check and relay endpoint failures that v0.1.2 added.
- E3.66 Connect onboarding, relay install and remote environment list: T3 Connect is T3's relay and
  Clerk accounts.
- E4.22 the T3 Connect switch, activity publishing and webhook hold: T3's relay and account service.

Peer links were never on this list, because they paired over the peer's own `/mcp` URL. v0.1.2
removed them, so their rows are listed as removed.

### Refused by rule

ADR 0007 refuses these, and the gateway renders each refusal the way t3code renders a missing
permission.

- **Rebase of a pushed branch** (A.89, and the `update-branch` action inside E3.51). It is a
  force-push, and Mend never force-pushes. t3code hides an update method absent from
  `viewerPermissions.updateMethods`.
- **A push or pull request by anyone but the change's owner** (E3.43b and E3.43c, and the same
  refusal inside A.137). The owner's push and create PR land since mend#1065. Scopes are per
  connection, so the control is live on a teammate's thread. The refusal is t3code's "Git action
  failed" toast carrying Mend's "only the change's owner lands it". A draft pull request is ADR
  0007's open question 3.

Merge, auto-merge and the merge settings left this list on 2026-10-24: Mend allows a merge a person
presses (decision 13), so they are a core change. Mend still never merges on its own.

## Decisions Yiannis made on 2026-10-24

Yiannis decided all thirteen open decisions on 2026-10-24 (Linear SLNT-86 to SLNT-98). Each names
the rows it settles. None is built yet unless a row says otherwise.

1. **Steering: real steering.** Build it in Mend first: an engine steer op for Codex and Claude
   (Codex `turn/steer {expectedTurnId}`, Claude a stream-json user message with priority `now`),
   `ProtocolHost.steerTurn` in `packages/sessions` and a Mend API route. Then hook T3 Code's
   steering up to it. Only the turn's sender may steer. The interrupt-and-send stopgap is not built.
   Rows: B.2, B.3, B.12, E2a.3, E2a.4, E2a.49.
2. **Model, effort and service tier on a started thread: all of it.** That includes a provider
   switch mid-thread ("100% T3 means 100% T3"). Live model and effort change first, then provider
   switching. A model picked on a started thread already applies at the agent's next start
   (mend#1066). Rows: the groups "Options on a live agent" and "Provider switch in one thread".
3. **Plan mode: yes, in 0.37, for Claude and Codex.** Rows: the group "Plan mode".
4. **Fork, rollback and merge back: yes.** Fork first, then rollback, which checkpoints before it
   resets, then merge back. SLNT-541 delivers the fork path with the limits in B.42 and E2b.40;
   rollback now saves recovery before native replacement and optional file restoration. Merge-back
   follows v0.1.2: save selected child context as a pending transfer to its direct parent, replace
   an earlier pending transfer from that child, and consume it once with the parent's next message.
   Merge back starts no turn, performs no Git merge and copies no files. Rows: the group "Fork,
   rollback and merge back".
5. **Delegated tasks and peer links: yes, in 0.37.** The orchestrator MCP for Mend's agents first,
   then the T3 Code part. Peer links are gone upstream at v0.1.2, so their rows are listed as
   removed. Rows: the group "Delegated tasks and the orchestrator MCP".
6. **Raw file reads: yes.** Shipped in mend#1139 and #1153. Rows A.123 and E3.34 remain PARTIAL for
   other resource kinds; the remaining default-branch reads are in "Project default-branch assets".
7. **Usage and limits: yes.** The usage summary, then limits, then reset credits, each on the
   asker's own login. Rows: the groups "Usage summary" and "Limits".
8. **Scratch projects and a new project by name: yes.** An empty repository in Mend's store, as a
   private project. Shipped in mend#1214. Rows: A.114, A.115, C.C18, E1a.28, E1a.35.
9. **Add a project from a path: not from a path on Mend's server.** Adding a project from a folder
   on the person's own machine is allowed. T3 Code sends only a path and the gateway runs on the
   server, so the repository has to come from the person's machine, as `mend handoff` does. The
   build designs that. Rows: A.119, E1a.31, E1a.32.
10. **Switch branch in a session's worktree: yes.** ADR 0007 is amended so the owner may switch
    branch in a worktree with one session and no landing in flight. Rows: A.143, A.144, B.17,
    E3.43g, E3.NG4.
11. **Delete the worktree too, and remove a project: yes.** Behind T3 Code's confirm dialogs. Mend's
    own refusals still apply: a live session, or a change not on origin. Removal shipped in
    mend#1227 and #1253. Rows: A.142, E1a.25, E1a.40. A.141 is standalone worktree creation.
12. **"Local" mode: yes.** One shared worktree per project on the default branch, not a session in
    the project checkout. The actual Git branch is `project.defaultBranch`, never `mend/local`.
    Lookup creates nothing; explicit start creates once and never resets it. An existing checkout
    holding the branch is refused without moving it. Rows: B.47, D.2, E2b.3, E2b.70.
13. **ADR 0012's "never" row.** Mend allows a merge when a person presses Merge in T3 Code. Mend
    still never merges on its own, including after automatic landing. Force-push stays refused. Only
    the change's owner lands. ADR 0012's "never" row splits into refused by rule (force-push, a
    landing by anyone but the change's owner), outside Mend (T3 Connect) and not yet (this matrix).
    This amends ADR 0007's "Mend never merges" for a merge a person presses. The ADR edits come in
    later changes. Rows: the group "Merge a person presses", A.89, E3.43b, E3.43c.

## §A. RPC methods

Each row is one method of `WsRpcGroup` at t3code v0.1.2: 198 methods. v0.1.1 had 199, the v0.0.46
pin's 188 plus 11 new. v0.1.2 removed the four `peerLinks.*` methods and added
`pullRequests.changeBreakdown`, `device.easAccount` and `projects.listIgnoredFiles`. None was
renamed. The t3code column cites `packages/contracts/src/rpc.ts` at v0.1.2 and the method's scope.
"Withheld" means the gateway does not grant the scope: `apps/t3-gateway/src/auth.ts:74-81` grants
`orchestration:read`, `orchestration:operate`, `terminal:read`, `terminal:operate`,
`source-control:write` (since mend#1065) and `filesystem:read`. The Gateway column cites
`apps/t3-gateway/src/rpc.ts`. Current gap evidence uses the Summary pin; unchanged supported cells
keep their historical references. `web` is t3code's `apps/web/src`. A feed that honestly answers
"none" is IMPLEMENTED, and so is a method no v0.1.2 client sends.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references | Removed at v0.1.2 |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 53          | 16      | 127     | 2           | 198         | 0                | 4                 |

Scope of the 143 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 26 gateway-only, 115 core, 1 rule; 1 graded per command
in §B.

Implemented 2026-11-07; box check pending.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

| **A.118** `shell.openInEditor`                  | rpc.ts:1261; `orchestration:operate`                 | Typed failure; operation is not served (`apps/t3-gateway/src/rpc.ts:1111-1112` at `f743fe3454`).                                                                                                                      | MISSING     | gateway-only: advertise remote open targets (VS Code Remote-SSH) from `GET /api/workspace-ssh` so the client opens locally | Deferred. T3 advertises one environment-level SSH host and username and opens paths directly, while Mend workspaces need a per-workspace SSH user plus a `/workspace/repo` mapping. Workaround: open the session in VS Code through Mend's own Remote-SSH (`mend code` or Mend's VS Code extension).                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## §B. Orchestration commands

The client union `OrchestrationV2Command` has 53 members at v0.1.2 (`N:2569-3014`) and 51 at v0.1.1
(`P:2650-3082`). v0.1.2 adds `queued-run.start` (`N:2889`, row B.V2) and moves `thread.stop`
(`N:2571`, row B.V1) into the client union from the internal one (`P:3187`). It removes no client
command. It drops the server-only `linkOrigin` and `delegatedFrom` from `thread.create`
(`P:2664-2666`), and adds an optional `repositoryWebUrl` to `thread.pull-request.link` and to the
link that `thread.pull-request.watch` carries (`N:2713, 2739`). The internal union loses
`delegated_task.remote.request`, `.complete` and `.stop` and `link_request.record`
(`P:3113-3160, 3213`), which no client sends. The earlier count of 55 types at v0.1.1 was wrong. The
49 rows group the 53 members, and each reaches the gateway through `orchestration.dispatchCommand`
(A.184). `N:` is t3code's `packages/contracts/src/orchestrationV2.ts` at v0.1.2 and `P:` the same
file at v0.1.1 (`v0.1.1-preview.20261017.3254`). `cmd:`, `launch:` and `proj:` are the gateway's
`commands.ts`, `launch.ts` and `thread-projection.ts`, and `cr:` is t3code's
`packages/client-runtime/src/operations/commands.ts` at v0.1.2. Current gap evidence uses the
Summary pin. Native steering, live model/effort choices and Plan/default interaction are
implemented. Fork is partial under SLNT-541. Rollback supports exact retained boundaries. Provider
switching remains open. Merge-back is implemented as a pending context transfer.

| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 35          | 6       | 8       | 0           | 49          | 0                | 0                 |

Scope of the 14 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 2 gateway-only, 12 core, 0 rule.

Claude 2.1.318 retains Plan, inline todos, session task files and persisted text output at the sealed boundary; native fake-provider probes verify isolated child resume and source deletion. Native subagents and unverified layouts refuse. Real pinned-client checks with fake logins passed for both providers; independent box check remains required. No fresh or summary fallback. |

## §C. Config, capability and auth fields

`C/` is `packages/contracts/src`, `CR/` is `packages/client-runtime/src`, `W/` is `apps/web/src` and
`S/` is `apps/server/src`, each at t3code v0.1.2 (044f07257c96). Bare file names are gateway files
in `apps/t3-gateway/src`. Current gap evidence uses the Summary pin. A field the client does not
read, or one whose absence is right for a headless Mend host, is IMPLEMENTED with that reason. A row
whose grade moved since the v0.1.1 matrix names the Mend pull request that moved it. Rows were
graded from source. A cell says "observed" only for what was run.

| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 79          | 9       | 34      | 3           | 125         | 0                | 2                 |

Scope of the 43 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 16 gateway-only, 26 core, 0 rule; 1 client limitation.

### ServerSettings the client reads

Contract `C/settings.ts:1130` onward. Gateway `model-preferences.ts:64-68`, t3code's defaults with
the person's model preferences. `server.getSettings` serves them (`rpc.ts:837-841`), and
`server.updateSettings` takes a patch of project actions alone or of model preferences alone and
refuses any other (`rpc.ts:845-881`).

## §D. Projection fields and turn-item kinds

`V2:a` is `packages/contracts/src/orchestrationV2.ts` line a at t3code v0.1.2. `OP:` is
`orchestrationProject.ts`, which v0.1.2 left unchanged. `web:`, `cr:`, `shared:`, `server:` and
`mobile:` are t3code's `apps/web/src`, `packages/client-runtime/src`, `packages/shared/src`,
`apps/server/src` and `apps/mobile/src` at v0.1.2. Gateway paths are `apps/t3-gateway/src` unless a
Mend package is named. Current gap evidence uses the Summary pin. The projection schema version is 2
in both t3code trees (server:`orchestration-v2/ProjectionStore.ts:593` at v0.1.2) and in the gateway
(`shell.ts:59`).

## §E. The web client

The E rows are controls in t3code's v0.1.2 web client. Each section names its own path keys.

### §E1. Sidebar, menus, palette and keybindings

In E1a, a bare path is t3code's `apps/web/src/`, `cr/` is `packages/client-runtime/src/`, `ct/` is
`packages/contracts/src/`, `G/` is Mend's `apps/t3-gateway/src/` and `A/` is Mend's
`packages/api-contracts/src/`. In E1b, `N/` and `O/` are `apps/web/src/` at v0.1.2 and at v0.1.1,
`Nc/` and `Ncr/` are v0.1.2's contracts and client runtime, and an `r` number is a row of the
2026-10-11 audit's E1. Every t3code path is read at `v0.1.2`. Current gap evidence uses the Summary
pin.

| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 52          | 6       | 10      | 0           | 68          | 32               | 1                 |

### §E2. Composer, thread view and launch

A t3code cell cites t3code v0.1.2 (044f07257c96). Current gap evidence uses the Mend pin in the
Summary. In E2a, a t3code path is under t3code's `apps/web/src/components/` unless it starts with
`apps/` or `packages/` (t3code's repository root) or `CR/` (`packages/client-runtime/src`), and `G/`
is the gateway's `apps/t3-gateway/src`. In E2b, a t3code path is under `apps/web/src/components/`
unless it starts with `apps/`, `packages/` or `CR/`, a Gateway path is under `apps/t3-gateway/src`,
and a bracketed number is a row of the 2026-10-11 audit's E2. Two E2b entry points are graded in
their own slices and are not rows here: the thread actions menu (the E1a menu rows) and thread
automations (E4.32).

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references | Removed at v0.1.2 |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 59          | 16      | 14      | 0           | 89          | 22               | 2                 |

Scope of the 30 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 7 gateway-only, 23 core, 0 rule.

### §E3. Right panel

`N:`, `NCR:`, `NC:` and `NSH:` are t3code's web app (`apps/web/src`), client runtime
(`packages/client-runtime/src`), contracts (`packages/contracts/src`) and shared package
(`packages/shared/src`) at v0.1.2. `G:` is the gateway (`apps/t3-gateway/src`) and `GT:` its tests
(`apps/t3-gateway/test`). `MC:` is Mend's `packages/api-contracts/src`, `ML:` is
`packages/landing/src`, `MS:` is `packages/store/src` and `MA:` is `apps/api/src`. E3.NG rows are
new in the git part and E3.NP rows new in the panel part (both at v0.1.1); E3.V rows are new at
v0.1.2.

| IMPLEMENTED | PARTIAL | MISSING | OUT-OF-MEND | Graded rows | Cross-references | Removed at v0.1.2 |
| ----------- | ------- | ------- | ----------- | ----------- | ---------------- | ----------------- |
| 27          | 17      | 42      | 1           | 87          | 8                | 0                 |

Scope of the 59 PARTIAL and MISSING rows: 27 gateway-only, 30 core, 2 rule.

#### Diff, git and pull requests

##### Right panel: Diff and pull-request tabs

Gates at v0.1.2: Diff needs `isServerThread && isGitRepo` (N:components/ChatView.tsx:3877,
`isGitRepo` from the `subscribeVcsStatus` `isRepo`, :1666-1672). The Pull request tab needs
`capabilities.pullRequests` and a target (:1186, :2434), and Linked needs `threadPullRequests` and a
visible link (:1890-1897). Both capabilities are absent (G:environment.ts:79-91). The shell sets
only `branch` and `worktreePath` (G:shell.ts:398-399, 635-636), never `linkedPullRequest`,
`pullRequests` or `branchPullRequest`. Since mend#1065 the VCS status's `pr` is the pull request a
landing recorded (G:landing.ts:114-139), which shows the sidebar and toolbar badges (E3.56) but
opens no tab.

checkpoint (N:DiffPanel.tsx:473-478; N:ChatView.tsx:1588-1593;

##### GitActionsControl and the branch toolbar

Only mount: N:components/chat/ThreadDetailsPanel.tsx:120-185 (needs `gitCwd`, N:ChatView.tsx:1573;
`BranchToolbar` when `isGitRepo`, `GitActionsControl` when the project has a name). Every write in
it is gated on `source-control:write` (`canWriteSourceControl`, N:GitActionsControl.tsx:975, 1148;
N:BranchToolbarBranchSelector.tsx:116, 159), which the gateway grants since mend#1065
(G:auth.ts:62-81; pairings stored without it get it once, G:state.ts:419-423, 631-633). So the
panel's actions now reach the gateway. Push and "Create PR" land the thread's change through Mend's
`POST /api/sessions/:id/land` (G:vcs.ts:419-498, G:landing.ts:156-181). Commit alone, a new branch,
a file selection, a typed message, pull, branch switch and create are refused in Mend's words
(G:landing.ts:144-154; G:rpc.ts:205-213, 1138, 1144-1145). The status that decides the labels comes
from Mend's change and its landing record (G:vcs.ts:44-74, 242-275): `hasWorkingTreeChanges` while
the change holds work no landing took (G:landing.ts:83-89), `workingTree.files` empty (G:vcs.ts:85,
96), `hasUpstream` once a landing pushed, `aheadCount` 1 while anything may be new, `behindCount` 0,
and `pr` the recorded pull request as GitHub last reported it (G:landing.ts:114-139). Labels and
gates are in N:GitActionsControl.logic.ts:118-337.

##### Pull requests

Code: `N:components/pullRequest/**`; the inbox moved from `N:routes/_chat.pull-requests.tsx` into
`N:components/pullRequest/PullRequestsPage.tsx` at v0.1.2. Every entry reads
`capabilities.pullRequests` / `threadPullRequests` / `threadPullRequestLinking` /
`threadPullRequestWatch` / `pullRequestStackActions` / `pullRequestChecks` (NC:environment.ts:84-85,
126-131), all absent (G:environment.ts:79-91), so no `pullRequests.*` RPC fires; the gateway's typed
failures (G:rpc.ts:973-1016) are defence in depth. The PR writes are client-guarded on
`source-control:write` (NC:clientRpcPermissions.ts:19-30), which the gateway grants since mend#1065,
so the capabilities alone keep them off. Mend's own PR facts come from landing (ADR 0007):
`ChangePullRequest` per change (number, url, state, title, head branch, observed time, `adopted`),
refreshed only on demand (`POST /api/landings/:id/refresh`, owner only) because "Mend does not poll
GitHub". The gateway asks for that refresh when the person refreshes the git panel, at most once a
minute per pull request (G:vcs.ts:213-240).

##### Files, file viewer, media, context chips

Code: `N:components/files/**`, `N:components/media/**`. Text reads use Mend's
`GET /api/worktrees/:id/contents?path=` (MC:worktrees.ts:104-116, 249): UTF-8 only, 1 MiB
(MA:routes/worktrees.ts:42), a path relative to the worktree (:388), `contents: null` for binary
(:408). **No Mend route returns a file's raw bytes**; the only binary response in the API is the git
bundle (MC:landing.ts:181-186). Every media row below waits on one core route. Decided 2026-10-24
(decision 6): build the raw read route (core S).

##### Preview / in-app browser

Code: `N:components/preview/**`, `N:browser/**`. With `preview:operate` withheld (G:auth.ts:62-81),
every entry below checks the scope first, so nothing reaches `preview.open` (refused,
G:rpc.ts:1210). Mend has Services (per-port TCP forwards, MC:sessions.ts:374-430,
`GET /api/services`, with a `browserUrl` in packages/domain/src/workbench/service.ts:110), not a

##### Usage and resource telemetry

Code: `N:components/usage/**`, `N:routes/usage.tsx`, `N:chat/useUsageLimitsPanel.ts`,
`N:chat/ComposerUsageLimits.tsx`, settings diagnostics. `diagnostics:read` withheld
(G:auth.ts:62-81). Mend records per-turn tokens (`AgentTurnUsage`,
packages/domain/src/workbench/agent-protocol.ts:30-37, `agent_turns.usage`) and serves them only per
session (`GET /api/sessions/:id/turns`, MC:sessions.ts:307); no cost, no totals, no rate-limit
windows. Decided 2026-10-24 (decision 7): usage summary, then limits, then reset credits, each on
the asker's own login (core).

## Method and evidence

This 2026-11-05 regrade checked all 421 rows that were PARTIAL or MISSING in the previous matrix
against the Summary's Mend commit and the pinned T3 Code source. It also checked the supported rows
affected by the merged work listed in the Summary. The source review split RPC/config,
commands/projection and client controls into separate audits, then reconciled their row evidence.

The current Gateway cell states the behavior found in source and cites the implementing route,
projection or refusal. A remaining gap keeps its grade even when another part of the same feature
has shipped. The audit corrected two overstated supported rows, command context C.E8 and approval
metadata D.89, and recognized the emitted provider-turn event D.E8. That regrade left 422 gap rows.
The current detailed rows, including later merged work, leave 377 gap rows.

A parser checked the 802 unique row ids, allowed grades and scope classes, then recomputed the
Summary and each section's counts from the detailed rows. Escaped table delimiters are treated as
cell content. For status-only scope cells, "box check pending" counts as gateway-only; agreed
deferrals, provider switching and native command discovery count as core. Mixed scopes count under
the first explicit scope label. The Summary groups describe related work, so a count of rows is not
a count of PRs.

This regrade did not run a server or client. Existing notes that describe a runtime observation or a
test remain historical evidence at their named revision. They are not new verification of main.
Previously supported rows outside the merged changes retain their historical citations.

The original matrix was built in mend#1062 at v0.1.1. SLNT-222 regraded the protocol and client for
v0.1.2 against Mend 95dcfb1b8. The current pin and evidence above supersede that source baseline.
